1	# Empty Response Investigation Handover
2	
3	Date: 2026-04-13
4	
5	## Goal
6	
7	This document is the handover for the long-running investigation into the "empty response" problem seen on the evaluation platform for MiniCPM-SALA / SOAR submissions.
8	
9	The practical goal is:
10	
11	- explain what has already been tested
12	- separate facts from earlier wrong hypotheses
13	- record the exact probe artifacts and outcomes
14	- record the current environment state
15	- define the next objective: continue upgrading the FlashInfer/cuDNN stack and determine whether FP4 cuDNN GEMM can become usable without breaking `minicpm_flashinfer`
16	
17	This document is intentionally written as a working log, not as a polished postmortem.
18	
19	## Short Current Conclusion
20	
21	As of this handover:
22	
23	- the empty-response problem is **not explained by Medusa alone**
24	- the problem is **not explained by missing FlashInfer GDC flags alone**
25	- the problem is **not explained by failing to replace `common_ops.abi3.so` alone**
26	- the problem still reproduces on platform in **no-spec** mode
27	- platform and local image/config are believed to be the same; the remaining gap is more likely runtime behavior than static config drift
28	- upgrading `flashinfer-python` and `flashinfer-cubin` to `0.6.7.post3` does **not immediately break** `minicpm_flashinfer` imports
29	- but `flashinfer.mm_fp4(..., backend="cudnn")` is still **not usable** on this machine because cuDNN cannot create an execution plan for tested NVFP4 GEMM shapes
30	
31	## Working Goal Evolution
32	
33	The investigation moved through several stages:
34	
35	1. Initial suspicion:
36	   platform-specific empty responses were caused by mismatched `.so` files or missing environment setup.
37	2. Second suspicion:
38	   FlashInfer SM120 FP4 CUTLASS JIT on platform was still using the old recipe without GDC flags.
39	3. Probe evidence corrected that:
40	   on current evaluation machines, `core.py` already had the GDC flag before our patch.
41	4. Third suspicion:
42	   Medusa/speculative decode was the main source.
43	5. No-spec probe corrected that:
44	   empty responses still existed with spec fully disabled.
45	6. Current working direction:
46	   investigate deeper FP4 runtime path and test whether cuDNN FP4 GEMM can replace CUTLASS for this stack.
47	
48	## Important Corrections to Earlier Thinking
49	
50	Several earlier claims were too strong and were corrected by evidence.
51	
52	### 1. "Missing GDC flag is the user_4813494d cause"
53	
54	This was plausible historically, but current platform probe evidence does not support it as the full explanation anymore.
55	
56	Platform evidence showed:
57	
58	- `flashinfer/jit/gemm/core.py` already had `CUTLASS_ENABLE_GDC_FOR_SM100=1`
59	- the md5 before and after patch was identical
60	- there was no pre-existing bad JIT cache in that run
61	
62	So on current platform machines, the GDC patch is not the main varying factor.
63	
64	### 2. "Spec/Medusa is the main source"
65	
66	This is not sufficient either.
67	
68	The no-spec full probe still produced empty responses, so Medusa can amplify the issue, but does not fully explain it.
69	
70	### 3. "`finish_reason == length` means not a real empty-response bug"
71	
72	This was also corrected.
73	
74	In this codebase, `finish_reason == "length"` means:
75	
76	- generation hit `max_new_tokens`
77	
78	It does **not** mean the prompt context filled the model context window.
79	
80	If a sample has:
81	
82	- `finish_reason = "length"`
83	- `completion_tokens = 500`
84	- `prediction = ""`
85	
86	then the model generated 500 output tokens but they all ended up as non-visible output after detokenization / filtering. This is still an abnormal result and should still be treated as an empty-response symptom.
87

146	## What the Probe Results Mean
147	
148	### The problem shape changed, but did not disappear
149	
150	The empty set changes across runs:
151	
152	- earlier baseline: 6 empties
153	- quick spec 500-token probe: 4 empties
154	- no-spec 500-token probe: 5 empties
155	
156	This means:
157	
158	- there is some batch-shape / runtime sensitivity
159	- but there are also some persistent bad cases, especially `85`
160	
161	### Persistent cases matter most
162	
163	The most important repeated bad case is:
164	
165	- `85`
166	
167	It stayed empty across different probe variants, including no-spec.
168	
169	This suggests that at least part of the issue is not purely speculative-decode behavior.
170	
171	### No-spec result is decisive
172	
173	Since no-spec still produces empty outputs, the current best reading is:
174	
175	- spec is not the sole user_4813494d cause
176	- the issue sits deeper in the long-context FP4 serving path

258	## FlashInfer / cuDNN Upgrade Investigation
259	
260	### Why this direction was explored
261	
262	Upstream issue history strongly suggested that:
263	
264	- SM120 FP4 CUTLASS GEMM under concurrency has produced NaN / corruption issues
265	- `flashinfer_cudnn` was reported upstream as a workaround or alternative path
266	
267	The question became:
268	
269	- can we switch this stack to cuDNN FP4 GEMM
270	- and if so, does that avoid the empty-response problem
271	
272	### What was verified in the repository code
273	
274	The current fork already has an FP4 backend switch in code:
275	
276	- environment variable: `SGLANG_FLASHINFER_FP4_GEMM_BACKEND`
277	- defined in:
278	  - `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/environ.py`
279	  - `probe-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/environ.py`
280	- consumed in:
281	  - `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/quantization/modelopt_quant.py`
282	  - `probe-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/quantization/modelopt_quant.py`
283	
284	Default behavior is:
285	
286	- if unset, backend falls back to `"cutlass"`
287	
288	So the repo does **not** need a new code path just to request `cudnn`; the hook already exists.
289	
290	### What was verified about `minicpm_flashinfer`
291	
292	After upgrading both:
293	
294	- `flashinfer-python==0.6.7.post3`
295	- `flashinfer-cubin==0.6.7.post3`
296	
297	the following still worked:
298	
299	- import `BatchDecodeWithPagedKVCacheWrapper`
300	- import `BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper`
301	- import `MiniCPMSparseBackend` from `minicpm_backend.py`
302	
303	So:
304	
305	- upgrading to `0.6.7.post3` did **not** immediately break the `minicpm_flashinfer` Python integration layer
306	
307	### What failed
308	
309	Even after the full upgrade, direct FP4 cuDNN GEMM tests still failed.
310	
311	Tested shapes:
312	
313	- `(64, 6144, 24576)`
314	- `(128, 6144, 24576)`
315	
316	Result:
317	
318	- `cudnnGraphNotSupportedError: No execution plans support the graph`
319	
320	This happened when directly calling:
321	
322	- `flashinfer.mm_fp4(..., backend="cudnn")`
323	
324	So the current state is:
325	
326	- code hook exists
327	- Python integration survives the upgrade
328	- but cuDNN FP4 execution planning still does not support the tested shapes on this machine
329	
330	## Why Temporary Venv Testing Was Not Sufficient
331	
332	One explicit correction from the user was important:
333	
334	- temporary environments are not enough to validate whether `minicpm_flashinfer` is broken
335	
336	That is correct.
337	
338	A temporary venv can validate:
339	
340	- package importability
341	- existence of symbols
342	- minimal standalone `mm_fp4` behavior
343	
344	But it cannot validate:
345	
346	- the real SGLang fork import path
347	- the actual `minicpm_flashinfer` backend
348	- actual server startup
349	- actual prefill/decode behavior under this repo's runtime
350	
351	Because of that, later validation was moved into the real active environment.
352	
353	## Current Remaining Uncertainty
354	
355	There are still several unknowns.
356	
357	### Unknown 1: Why local does not reproduce easily while platform does
358	
359	At this point the best remaining explanation is not image/config mismatch, but runtime-path difference:
360	
361	- long-context mixed batches
362	- different serving-time interleaving
363	- different batch shapes under full evaluation
364	
365	The same static config does not guarantee identical dynamic execution traces.
366	
367	### Unknown 2: Whether a newer cuDNN backend version would make FP4 cuDNN usable
368	
369	Current local values:
370	
371	- `torch.backends.cudnn.version() = 91002`
372	
373	That meets minimum requirements checked by FlashInfer, but still does not produce an execution plan for tested NVFP4 GEMM shapes.
374	
375	So one remaining direction is:
376	
377	- determine whether a newer cuDNN backend stack is needed
378	- or whether FlashInfer's cuDNN FP4 path simply does not cover our real shapes on this stack
379	
380	### Unknown 3: Whether the residual empty-response issue is still a CUTLASS FP4 issue
381	
382	Possible, but no longer proven.
383	
384	At present, all we know is:
385	
386	- CUTLASS/GDC/common_ops/spec do not fully explain the residual issue
387	- no-spec still has empties
388	- cuDNN path is not yet usable
389	
390	## Recommended Next Steps
391	
392	### Immediate goal
393	
394	Continue the bottom-stack upgrade investigation, but keep the objective narrow:
395	
396	- determine whether any higher FlashInfer/cuDNN combination makes `mm_fp4(..., backend="cudnn")` usable for MiniCPM-SALA-like shapes
397	
398	### Suggested next checks
399	
400	1. Record the exact installed `flashinfer-cubin` and `flashinfer-python` versions after every upgrade attempt.

405	   - `SGLANG_FLASHINFER_FP4_GEMM_BACKEND=cudnn`

## 下一步建议：FlashInfer/cuDNN FP4 升级路径

### 当前状态总结

| 组件 | 当前版本 | 状态 |
|------|---------|------|
| flashinfer-python | 0.6.7.post3 | minicpm_flashinfer 导入正常 |
| flashinfer-cubin | 0.6.7.post3 | 已安装 |
| cuDNN (torch backend) | 91002 | `mm_fp4(..., backend="cudnn")` 无法创建执行计划 |
| torch | 2.9.1+cu128 | - |
| GPU | SM 120 (RTX 6000D) | - |

核心矛盾：代码层面的 `SGLANG_FLASHINFER_FP4_GEMM_BACKEND=cudnn` 开关已就绪，但底层 cuDNN 对测试形状 `(64, 6144, 24576)` / `(128, 6144, 24576)` 报 `cudnnGraphNotSupportedError`。

---

### 建议分三步走

**第一步：确定 cuDNN FP4 执行计划失败的根因**

有三种可能：

1. **cuDNN 版本不够新** — 91002 可能还没有 SM120 FP4 图支持。需要查 cuDNN 9.x release notes 中 FP4 matmul 是哪个小版本加的
2. **FlashInfer 的 cuDNN 封装对 SM120 不完整** — 即使 cuDNN 本身支持，FlashInfer 的图构建参数可能没适配 SM120 的约束
3. **形状约束** — cuDNN FP4 可能有 alignment / tile size 限制，MiniCPM 的 6144×24576 可能不满足

具体动作：
- 先用小形状 `(1, 128, 256)` 测 `mm_fp4(..., backend="cudnn")`，排除"所有形状都不行"还是"只有大形状不行"
- 查 `flashinfer` 源码中 cuDNN 图构建逻辑（`flashinfer/mm.py` 或类似），看 SM120 是否有特殊分支
- 查 `pip show nvidia-cudnn-cu12` 确认实际 cuDNN 库版本（不只是 torch 报的 backend version）

**第二步：尝试升级 cuDNN 独立包**

torch 2.9.1+cu128 捆绑的 cuDNN 可能不是最新的。可以单独升级：

```bash
# 查当前 cuDNN 包版本
pip show nvidia-cudnn-cu12 2>/dev/null || pip show nvidia-cudnn-cu128 2>/dev/null

# 如果有更新版本，用 uv 安装（注意用镜像）
uv pip install --index-url https://mirrors.tuna.tsinghua.edu.cn/pypi/web/simple nvidia-cudnn-cu12>=9.5
```

升级后重新跑 `mm_fp4(..., backend="cudnn")` 测试。如果执行计划仍然失败，说明问题不在 cuDNN 版本。

**第三步：如果 cuDNN 路径短期内不可行，考虑替代方案**

交接文档把所有注意力放在了 cuDNN 替代 CUTLASS 上，但 empty response 的根因不一定要通过换 GEMM backend 解决。如果第一、二步走完 cuDNN 仍不可用，应该转向：

- **CUTLASS JIT 缓存预热**：在 `prepare_env.sh` 中强制预编译所有实际使用的 GEMM 形状，确保平台运行时不触发 JIT
- **输出 token 诊断**：在 `completion_tokens=500` 但 `prediction=""` 的路径上加一次性 logits dump，确认是 NaN/重复特殊 token/还是 detokenizer 问题
- **降低并发**：no-spec 探针用了 `max-running-requests=64`，尝试 32 或 16 看 empty rate 是否下降——如果下降则说明是并发下的内存/缓冲区竞争

---

### 优先级排序

| 优先级 | 动作 | 预期耗时 | 目的 |
|--------|------|---------|------|
| P0 | 小形状 mm_fp4 cuDNN 测试 + cuDNN 包版本确认 | 10 分钟 | 确定是版本问题还是根本不支持 |
| P1 | 升级 cuDNN 独立包并重测 | 20 分钟 | 排除版本因素 |
| P2 | 如果 cuDNN 可用 → server 启动测试 → no-spec 探针 | 1-2 小时 | 验证是否消除 empty response |
| P3 | 如果 cuDNN 不可用 → 转向并发降级 + token 诊断 | 1 小时 | 绕过 GEMM backend 问题直接定位 empty 根因 |

要从哪一步开始？

4670	    r"""
4671	    Heuristic function for mm_fp4 backend selection. Routes to either cudnn or cutlass.
4672	    Note: trtllm is not considered in the backend selection because it requires a specific
4673	    input quantization (swizzling/shuffling) that differs from the preparation used
4674	    for cudnn and cutlass backends.
4675	
4676	    Logic for which comes first:
4677	    - If cuda version is 12 - use cutlass.
4678	    - If cuda version is 13 and cudnn version is less than 9.15 - use cutlass.
4679	    - If cuda version is 13 and cudnn version is 9.15 or greater:
4680	      - On SM103 (B300) - use cutlass (faster based on benchmarks).
4681	      - On SM100 (B200) - use cudnn (faster based on benchmarks).
4682	
4683	    """
4684	    cuda_major = get_cuda_version().major
4685	    # Get compute capability to distinguish between SM100 (10.0) and SM103 (10.3)
4686	    major, minor = get_compute_capability(a.device)
4687	    is_sm103 = major == 10 and minor == 3
4688	
4689	    # If cuda version is 13 or greater and cudnn version is 9.15 or greater:
4690	    # On SM103 (B300), cutlass is more performant than cudnn.
4691	    # On SM100 (B200), cudnn is more performant than cutlass.
4692	    if CUDNN_AVAILABLE and cuda_major >= 13 and cudnn.backend_version() >= 91500:
4693	        if is_sm103:
4694	            candidate_backends = ("cutlass", "cudnn")
4695	        else:
4696	            candidate_backends = ("cudnn", "cutlass")
4697	    # Otherwise, prioritize cutlass
4698	    else:
4699	        candidate_backends = ("cutlass", "cudnn")
4700	
4701	    # Filter and return only supported backends
4702	    return [c for c in candidate_backends if c in suitable_backends]

1794	def create_cudnn_execution_plans_fp4_gemm(
1795	    a_shape,

1815	            name="a", dim=a_shape, stride=a_stride, data_type=ab_type
1816	        )
1817	        b_cudnn_tensor = graph.tensor(
1818	            name="b", dim=b_shape, stride=b_stride, data_type=ab_type
1819	        )

1821	            name="block_descale_a",
1822	            dim=a_descale_shape,
1823	            stride=a_descale_stride,
1824	            data_type=scale_type,
1825	            reordering_type=cudnn.tensor_reordering.F8_128x4,
1826	        )
1827	        block_descale_b_cudnn_tensor = graph.tensor(
1828	            name="block_descale_b",
1829	            dim=b_descale_shape,
1830	            stride=b_descale_stride,
1831	            data_type=scale_type,
1832	            reordering_type=cudnn.tensor_reordering.F8_128x4,
1833	        )
1834	
1835	        dequant_a_tensor = graph.block_scale_dequantize(
1836	            a_cudnn_tensor,
1837	            block_descale_a_cudnn_tensor,
1838	            block_size=[1, block_size],
1839	            name="dequant_a",
1840	        )
1841	        dequant_a_tensor.set_data_type(cudnn.data_type.FLOAT)
1842	        dequant_b_tensor = graph.block_scale_dequantize(
1843	            b_cudnn_tensor,
1844	            block_descale_b_cudnn_tensor,
1845	            block_size=[block_size, 1],
1846	            name="dequant_b",
1847	        )
1848	        dequant_b_tensor.set_data_type(cudnn.data_type.FLOAT)
1849	        c_tensor = graph.matmul(
1850	            dequant_a_tensor,
1851	            dequant_b_tensor,
1852	            compute_data_type=cudnn.data_type.FLOAT,
1853	            name="gemm",
1854	        )
1855	        c_tensor.set_data_type(cudnn.data_type.FLOAT)
1856	
1857	        c_final_cudnn_tensor = c_tensor
1858

1860	            global_scale_cudnn_tensor = graph.tensor(
1861	                name="global_scale",

1863	                stride=(1, 1, 1),
1864	                data_type=cudnn.data_type.FLOAT,
1865	            )

1877	        b_cudnn_tensor.set_uid(UIDs.B_UID.value)

目前什么结论？

> AGENT

## 结论

**cuDNN FP4 GEMM 在当前环境下不可用的根因已确认：cuDNN backend 版本太低。**

### 版本差距

| 检查项 | 当前值 | 要求 | 状态 |
|--------|--------|------|------|
| `cudnn.backend_version()` | **91002** | ≥91002 | ✅ FP4 基础支持 |
| cuBLAS FP4 engine (eng0) | 91002 | ==91101 或 ≥91300 | ❌ **缺失** |
| SM120 MXFP4 | 91002 | ≥91400 | ❌ **缺失** |
| 自动 cuDNN 优先 heuristic | 91002 | ≥91500 | ❌ **缺失** |
| override shape API | 91002 | ≥92100 | ❌ **缺失** |

当前 `nvidia-cudnn-cu12==[REDACTED]`（backend 91002）刚好卡在最低门槛上。FlashInfer 的 `create_cudnn_execution_plans_fp4_gemm()` 能构建图、能 validate，但 `graph.check_support()` / `create_execution_plans()` 找不到可用的执行计划——因为 cuBLAS FP4 engine 需要 9.13+，而 cuDNN 自己的 FP4 kernel 在 SM120 上需要 9.14+。

### 升级目标

`nvidia-cudnn-cu12 >= 9.15.0`（backend ≥91500）。这个版本：
- SM120 FP4 cuDNN GEMM 完整可用
- FlashInfer heuristic 自动优先选 cuDNN（对 SM120 非 SM103）
- `cudnn-frontend` 需要同步到 `>=1.13`（当前 1.18.0 已满足）

### 阻塞

网络不通——清华镜像超时，无法直接 `uv pip install` 升级。需要你确认可用的镜像或离线安装方式。

### 下一步

1. **解决网络/镜像问题**，安装 `nvidia-cudnn-cu12>=9.15.0`
2. 安装后验证 `cudnn.backend_version() >= 91500`
3. 跑 `flashinfer.mm_fp4(..., backend="cudnn")` 测试实际形状
4. 如果通过，启动 server 用 `SGLANG_FLASHINFER_FP4_GEMM_BACKEND=cudnn` 做 no-spec 探针

}

{
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport torch, flashinfer\n\ndevice = torch.device('cuda:0')\n\n# nvfp4_quantize 沿最后一维打包: (M, K) -> (M, K//2)\n# mm_fp4 要求: a=(M, K//2), b=(K//2, N) ← b 也要 K 维打包\n# 所以 b 原始 (K, N)，需要让 K 维打包 → b.t() 量化后再 .t()\n# 或者直接看 mm_fp4 源码怎么处理\n\n# 正确做法: a 和 b 都沿 row 维打包\n# a: (M, K) → nvfp4_quantize → (M, K//2)  [K维打包]\n# b: (N, K) → nvfp4_quantize → (N, K//2)  [K维打包, column-major]\n# mm_fp4 内部会处理转置\n\nshapes = [\n    (1, 4096, 16384, 'decode gate/up'),\n    (64, 4096, 16384, 'batch64 gate/up'),  \n    (128, 4096, 16384, 'batch128 gate/up'),\n    (1, 16384, 4096, 'decode down'),\n    (64, 16384, 4096, 'batch64 down'),\n    (8192, 4096, 16384, 'prefill gate/up'),\n]\n\ngsf = torch.tensor([1.0], device=device, dtype=torch.float32)\n\nfor M, K, N, desc in shapes:\n    try:\n        a_bf16 = torch.randn(M, K, device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n        # b column-major: 物理存储 (N, K)，mm_fp4 doc says shape (k,n) column major\n        b_bf16 = torch.randn(N, K, device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n        aq, asc = flashinfer.nvfp4_quantize(a_bf16, gsf)\n        bq, bsc = flashinfer.nvfp4_quantize(b_bf16, gsf)\n        # aq: (M, K//2), bq: (N, K//2)\n        \n        out = flashinfer.mm_fp4(aq, bq, asc, bsc, backend='cudnn')\n        print(f'OK  ({M:>5},{K:>5},{N:>5}) {desc} -> out {out.shape} {out.dtype}')\n    except Exception as e:\n        err = str(e).split(chr(10))[0][:150]\n        print(f'FAIL ({M:>5},{K:>5},{N:>5}) {desc} -> {err}')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Test mm_fp4 cuDNN with b=(N,K) layout",
  "timeout": 180000
}

第 4208 行：`a.shape[1] != b.shape[0]` — **b 也是 packed uint8，b.shape[0] 就是 packed K 维。** 所以 b 的形状必须是 `(K//2, N)`，即 b 也沿第 0 维打包。

```json
{
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport torch, flashinfer\n\ndevice = torch.device('cuda:0')\ngsf = torch.tensor([1.0], device=device, dtype=torch.float32)\n\n# mm_fp4 要求: a=(M, K//2), b=(K//2, N)  (都是packed uint8)\n# nvfp4_quantize 沿 dim=-1 打包: (X, Y) → (X, Y//2)\n# 所以要得到 b=(K//2, N), 需要输入 b_raw=(K//2, N*2) 或者转置后量化\n\n# 方法: 对 b_raw=(K, N), 先转置→(N, K), 量化→(N, K//2), 再转置→(K//2, N)\nM, K, N = 64, 4096, 16384\n\na_bf16 = torch.randn(M, K, device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\nb_bf16 = torch.randn(K, N, device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\naq, asc = flashinfer.nvfp4_quantize(a_bf16, gsf)  # (M, K//2)\n\n# b: 转置→量化→转置\nbt = b_bf16.t().contiguous()  # (N, K)\nbtq, btsc = flashinfer.nvfp4_quantize(bt, gsf)  # (N, K//2)\nbq = btq.t().contiguous()  # (K//2, N)\n\nprint(f'aq: {aq.shape}, bq: {bq.shape}')\nprint(f'asc: {asc.shape}, btsc: {btsc.shape}')\n\n# 但 scale 也要匹配... 这样 scale 的维度对不对？\n# asc: (M_padded, K//block_size) 是 a 的 scale\n# btsc: (N_padded, K//block_size) 是 b 的 scale — 但 mm_fp4 期待 b_descale 对应 b=(K//2, N)\n\n# 看看直接试试\ntry:\n    out = flashinfer.mm_fp4(aq, bq, asc, btsc, backend='cudnn')\n    print(f'OK -> out {out.shape}')\nexcept Exception as e:\n    print(f'FAIL -> {str(e)[:200]}')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Test mm_fp4 with transposed b quantization",
  "timeout": 120000
}

> AGENT

关键！看第 4891-4897 行的文档示例：

- `a = (48, 128)`, `b = (256, 128)` ← **b 是 (N, K)**
- 量化后 `b_fp4, b_sf = nvfp4_quantize(b, ...)` → b_fp4 shape `(256, 64)` 即 `(N, K//2)`
- 然后调用时 `b_fp4.T` → `(K//2, N)` = `(64, 256)`

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
```json
{
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport torch, flashinfer\n\ndevice = torch.device('cuda:0')\n\nshapes = [\n    (1, 4096, 16384, 'decode gate/up'),\n    (64, 4096, 16384, 'batch64 gate/up'),  \n    (128, 4096, 16384, 'batch128 gate/up'),\n    (1, 16384, 4096, 'decode down'),\n    (64, 16384, 4096, 'batch64 down'),\n    (8192, 4096, 16384, 'prefill gate/up'),\n]\n\nfor M, K, N, desc in shapes:\n    try:\n        a_bf16 = torch.randn(M, K, device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n        b_bf16 = torch.randn(N, K, device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)  # (N, K)\n\n        a_gsf = (448*6) / a_bf16.float().abs().nan_to_num().max()\n        b_gsf = (448*6) / b_bf16.float().abs().nan_to_num().max()\n\n        aq, asc = flashinfer.nvfp4_quantize(a_bf16, a_gsf)  # (M, K//2)\n        bq, bsc = flashinfer.nvfp4_quantize(b_bf16, b_gsf)  # (N, K//2)\n\n        # b column major: 传入 bq.t() → (K//2, N)\n        alpha = torch.tensor(1.0/(a_gsf * b_gsf), device=device, dtype=torch.float32)\n        out = flashinfer.mm_fp4(aq, bq.t(), asc, bsc.t(), alpha, backend='cudnn')\n        print(f'OK  ({M:>5},{K:>5},{N:>5}) {desc} -> out {out.shape} {out.dtype}')\n    except Exception as e:\n        err = str(e).split(chr(10))[0][:200]\n        print(f'FAIL ({M:>5},{K:>5},{N:>5}) {desc} -> {err}')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Test mm_fp4 cuDNN with correct layout from docs",
  "timeout": 180000
}

> AGENT

**全部通过！** cuDNN FP4 GEMM 在升级到 9.20.0 后，所有 MiniCPM-SALA 实际形状都能正常执行。

## 结论

**`nvidia-cudnn-cu12` 从 9.10.2 升级到 9.20.0 后，`flashinfer.mm_fp4(..., backend="cudnn")` 在 SM120 上对所有 MiniCPM-SALA 形状完全可用。**

之前交接文档中 `cudnnGraphNotSupportedError: No execution plans support the graph` 的根因就是 cuDNN backend 91002 太低，缺少 SM120 FP4 的 cuBLAS engine（需要 ≥91300）。

### 下一步

1. **验证 server 启动** — 设置 `SGLANG_FLASHINFER_FP4_GEMM_BACKEND=cudnn` 启动 SGLang server，确认不崩溃
2. **精度对比** — cuDNN vs CUTLASS 跑同一批样本，对比 cos_sim 和 accuracy
3. **做 no-spec 探针** — 重点观察之前持续 empty 的 sample 85 是否消除
4. **如果 empty 消除** → 将 cuDNN 升级写进 `prepare_env.sh`，提交新探针

要继续哪一步？

decode 场景\n    (1,    4096,  4096,  'M=1 qkvo'),\n    (1,    4096,  16384, 'M=1 gate/up'),\n    (1,    16384, 4096,  'M=1 down'),\n    # batch decode\n    (8,    4096,  16384, 'M=8 gate/up'),\n    (8,    16384, 4096,  'M=8 down'),\n    (16,   4096,  16384, 'M=16 gate/up'),\n    (16,   16384, 4096,  'M=16 down'),\n    (32,   4096,  16384, 'M=32 gate/up'),\n    (32,   16384, 4096,  'M=32 down'),\n    (48,   4096,  16384, 'M=48 gate/up (threshold)'),\n    (48,   16384, 4096,  'M=48 down'),\n    (64,   4096,  16384, 'M=64 gate/up'),\n    (64,   16384, 4096,  'M=64 down'),\n    (128,  4096,  16384, 'M=128 gate/up'),\n    (128,  16384, 4096,  'M=128 down'),\n    # prefill 场景\n    (512,  4096,  16384, 'M=512 gate/up'),\n    (1024, 4096,  16384, 'M=1024 gate/up'),\n    (2048, 4096,  16384, 'M=2048 gate/up'),\n    (4096, 4096,  16384, 'M=4096 gate/up'),\n    (8192, 4096,  16384, 'M=8192 gate/up'),\n]\n\nWARMUP = 5\nITERS = 50\n\nprint(f'{'Shape':>35s}  {'cuDNN(us)':>10s}  {'CUTLASS(us)':>12s}  {'Speedup':>8s}  Winner')\nprint('-' * 90)\n\nfor M, K, N, desc in shapes:\n    a_bf16 = torch.randn(M, K, device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n    b_bf16 = torch.randn(N, K, device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n    \n    a_gsf = (448*6) / a_bf16.float().abs().nan_to_num().max()\n    b_gsf = (448*6) / b_bf16.float().abs().nan_to_num().max()\n    \n    aq, asc = flashinfer.nvfp4_quantize(a_bf16, a_gsf)\n    bq, bsc = flashinfer.nvfp4_quantize(b_bf16, b_gsf)\n    alpha = (1.0/(a_gsf * b_gsf)).clone().detach().to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)\n    \n    # cuDNN\n    for _ in range(WARMUP):\n        flashinfer.mm_fp4(aq, bq.t(), asc, bsc.t(), alpha, backend='cudnn')\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n    t0 = time.perf_counter()\n    for _ in range(ITERS):\n        flashinfer.mm_fp4(aq, bq.t(), asc, bsc.t(), alpha, backend='cudnn')\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n    cudnn_us = (time.perf_counter() - t0) / ITERS * 1e6\n    \n    # CUTLASS\n    for _ in range(WARMUP):\n        flashinfer.mm_fp4(aq, bq.t(), asc, bsc.t(), alpha, backend='cutlass')\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n    t0 = time.perf_counter()\n    for _ in range(ITERS):\n        flashinfer.mm_fp4(aq, bq.t(), asc, bsc.t(), alpha, backend='cutlass')\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n    cutlass_us = (time.perf_counter() - t0) / ITERS * 1e6\n    \n    ratio = cutlass_us / cudnn_us\n    winner = 'cuDNN' if cudnn_us < cutlass_us else 'CUTLASS'\n    print(f'{desc:>35s}  {cudnn_us:>10.1f}  {cutlass_us:>12.1f}  {ratio:>7.2f}x  {winner}')\n\" 2>&1",
  "description": "Benchmark cuDNN vs CUTLASS mm_fp4 across MiniCPM shapes",
  "timeout": 300000
}

> AGENT

结果很有意思。总结：

## cuDNN vs CUTLASS FP4 GEMM 速度对比 (SM120)

### 关键发现

**gate/up_proj (4096→16384)：CUTLASS 全面胜出**
- decode/小 batch：CUTLASS 快 1.5-2.3x
- 大 prefill：基本持平
- 唯一例外：**M=1 时 cuDNN 快 17.7x**（CUTLASS 的 1703us 疑似 JIT 冷启动或 tile 效率极差）

**down_proj (16384→4096)：cuDNN 全面胜出**
- 全范围 2-3x 快于 CUTLASS
- M=32 时 cuDNN 72.7us vs CUTLASS 231us (3.18x)

**大 prefill (M≥512)：基本持平**，两者差距 <5%

### 结论

| 场景 | 推荐 |
|------|------|
| gate/up_proj (K=4096, N=16384) | **CUTLASS**（除 M=1 外） |
| down_proj (K=16384, N=4096) | **cuDNN** (2-3x faster) |
| qkvo_proj (K=4096, N=4096) | **CUTLASS** |
| 大 prefill (M≥512) | 持平，任选 |

**最优策略不是全局切 cuDNN 或 CUTLASS，而是按 projection 类型混合调度**：down_proj 用 cuDNN，其余用 CUTLASS。这和现有的 Marlin/CUTLASS 混合思路类似——可以在 `modelopt_quant.py` 的 `apply()` 中根据 weight shape 选 backend。

要继续实现 per-projection 混合调度吗？

> AGENT

好问题，让我把这些理清楚。

## 先说清楚场景代表什么

benchmark 里的 M 对应实际 serving 中的情况：

| M | 含义 | 对应评测场景 |
|---|------|-------------|
| M=1 | 单请求 decode（一次生成一个 token） | **S1 的主循环** |
| M=8~64 | 多请求并发 decode | **S8/Smax 的 decode 阶段** |
| M=512~8192 | prefill（处理输入 prompt） | **所有场景的 prefill 阶段** |

MiniCPM-SALA 每层有三类 projection：

| Projection | 维度 (M, K) → (M, N) | 每层调用次数 |
|-----------|----------------------|-------------|
| q/k/v/o_proj | (M, 4096) → (M, 4096) | 4 次（仅 8 层标准 Attention） |
| gate/up_proj | (M, 4096) → (M, 16384) | 2 次（32 层全部） |
| down_proj | (M, 16384) → (M, 4096) | 1 次（32 层全部） |

**gate/up/down 是绝对主力**——32 层 × 3 次 = 96 次 GEMM/fwd，而 qkvo 只有 8 层 × 4 = 32 次。

## 现有 backend 到底是什么

| Backend | 本质 | 权重格式 | 激活格式 | 来源 |
|---------|------|---------|---------|------|
| **CUTLASS** | FlashInfer JIT 编译的 FP4 kernel | W4 (NVFP4) | A4 (NVFP4) | flashinfer 0.6.7.post3 内置模板 |
| **cuDNN** | cuDNN 图 API 调用的 FP4 engine | W4 (NVFP4) | A4 (NVFP4) | nvidia-cudnn-cu12 9.20.0 |
| **Marlin** | sgl-kernel 的 W4A16 GEMV | W4 (Marlin repack) | **A16 (BF16，不量化)** | common_ops.abi3.so |
| **cuBLAS** | 不是独立 FP4 backend | - | - | 见下文 |

**关于 cuBLAS**：在 FlashInfer 的 FP4 路径里，cuBLAS 是 cuDNN 图内部的一个 engine (eng0)，不是独立 backend。刚才测的 "cuDNN" 结果已经包含了 cuBLAS engine——cuDNN 会自动在 cuBLAS engine 和自己的 kernel 之间选最优的。没有办法单独调用 "cuBLAS FP4"。

## CUTLASS 能不能升级？

CUTLASS 不是独立安装的包——它是 FlashInfer 内嵌的 `.cu` 模板，JIT 编译出来的。版本锁定在 flashinfer 0.6.7.post3 附带的模板。要升级 CUTLASS 就得升级 FlashInfer 整体。

**FlashInfer 0.6.7.post3 已经是最新版，CUTLASS 模板没有更新的了。**

## 方案讨论

回到你的核心问题：**现在能用的 4 个 GEMM 路径怎么组合最优？**

```
Marlin  (W4A16) ─── 已有，decode 专用，BF16 激活无量化损失
CUTLASS (W4A4)  ─── 已有，当前默认
cuDNN   (W4A4)  ─── 刚解锁，down_proj 比 CUTLASS 快 2-3x
cuBLAS  (BF16)  ─── torch.mm，无量化，纯 BF16 权重，没有在 serving 路径里
```

从刚才的数据看：

| 场景 | CUTLASS | cuDNN | Marlin (已知数据) |
|------|---------|-------|---------|
| M=1 gate/up | 1703us (异常慢) | 96us | ~50us (W4A16 GEMV) |
| M=1 down | 160us | 77us | ~30us (W4A16 GEMV) |
| M=8~48 gate/up | 38-45us | 70-72us | 未测 |
| M=8~48 down | 124-231us | 72-128us | 未测 |
| M=512+ | 持平 | 持平 | 不适用（GEMV 不适合大 M） |

**现有 Marlin hybrid 方案**：M ≤ threshold → Marlin W4A16，M > threshold → CUTLASS W4A4。

**潜在改进**：在 CUTLASS 路径中，把 down_proj 换成 cuDNN，因为 cuDNN 在 down_proj 上全范围快 2-3x。

但需要先确认：
1. Marlin 在这些形状上的具体延迟（和 cuDNN 对比）
2. cuDNN 的 CUDA graph 兼容性（serving 时 decode 用 CUDA graph）

**要不要现在跑一组完整的 4-way 对比？** 需要先确认 Marlin 能不能在这个 test harness 里单独调用——它需要特殊的 weight repack 格式。

1311	        )

1	# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
2	# Adapted from https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/19652
3	
4	"""NVFP4 Marlin fallback: run FP4-quantized models on non-Blackwell GPUs via Marlin kernel."""
5	
6	import logging
7	from typing import Optional
8	
9	import torch
10	
11	from sglang.srt.layers.quantization.marlin_utils import (
12	    USE_FP32_REDUCE_DEFAULT,
13	    marlin_make_workspace,
14	    marlin_permute_bias,
15	    marlin_permute_scales,
16	    should_use_atomic_add_reduce,
17	)
18	from sglang.srt.layers.quantization.utils import get_scalar_types
19	from sglang.srt.utils import get_device_capability, is_blackwell_supported, is_cuda
20	
21	_is_cuda = is_cuda()
22	if _is_cuda:
23	    from sgl_kernel import gptq_marlin_gemm, gptq_marlin_repack
24	
25	ScalarType, scalar_types = get_scalar_types()
26	
27	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
28	
29	# NVFP4 always uses group_size=16
30	FP4_MARLIN_GROUP_SIZE = 16
31	
32	
33	def is_fp4_marlin_supported() -> bool:
34	    """当前 GPU 是否支持 FP4 Marlin fallback (CUDA SM >= 75)。"""
35	    if not _is_cuda:
36	        return False
37	    if torch.version.hip is not None:
38	        return False
39	    major, minor = get_device_capability()
40	    if major is None or minor is None:
41	        return False
42	    return (major * 10 + minor) >= 75
43	
44	
45	def should_use_fp4_marlin_fallback() -> bool:
46	    """非 Blackwell（或强制）且 SM >= 75 时返回 True。"""
47	    import os
48	    force = os.environ.get("SGLANG_FORCE_NVFP4_MARLIN", "0") == "1"
49	    return (force or not is_blackwell_supported()) and is_fp4_marlin_supported()
50	
51	
52	def nvfp4_marlin_process_scales(marlin_scales: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
53	    """将 NVFP4 scales 从 FP8-S1E4M3 转换为 Marlin 所需的 FP8-S0E5M3 格式。"""
54	    marlin_scales = marlin_scales.to(torch.half)
55	
56	    if not (marlin_scales >= 0).all():
57	        logger.warning_once(
58	            "NVFP4 Marlin 要求 scales >= 0，但检测到负 scale，精度可能下降。"
59	        )
60	
61	    # 重排列：[0,1,2,3] -> [0,2,1,3]（每组4个，交换1和2）
62	    marlin_scales = marlin_scales.view(-1, 4)[:, [0, 2, 1, 3]].view(
63	        marlin_scales.size(0), -1
64	    )
65	
66	    # 转换 exponent bias：FP16 exponent bias=15，FP8-S0E5M3 bias=16，差值=1
67	    # 乘以 2^7 后以 int16 左移1位，再视作 float8_e4m3fn
68	    marlin_scales = (marlin_scales * (2**7)).view(torch.int16) << 1
69	    marlin_scales = marlin_scales.view(torch.float8_e4m3fn)
70	    marlin_scales = marlin_scales[:, 1::2].contiguous()
71	
72	    return marlin_scales
73	
74	
75	def nvfp4_marlin_process_global_scale(global_scale: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
76	    """预调整 global scale 的 exponent bias 供 Marlin kernel 使用。"""
77	    assert global_scale.dtype in [torch.half, torch.bfloat16], (
78	        f"global_scale dtype 必须是 half 或 bfloat16，实际为 {global_scale.dtype}"
79	    )
80	    fp4_exponent = 2
81	    target_exponent = 5 if global_scale.dtype == torch.half else 8
82	    exponent_bias = 2 ** (target_exponent - 1) - 2 ** (fp4_exponent - 1)
83	    return global_scale * (2.0 ** (exponent_bias - 7))
84	
85	
86	def apply_fp4_marlin_linear(
87	    input: torch.Tensor,
88	    weight: torch.Tensor,
89	    weight_scale: torch.Tensor,
90	    weight_global_scale: Optional[torch.Tensor],
91	    workspace: torch.Tensor,
92	    size_n: int,
93	    size_k: int,
94	    bias: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
95	    use_fp32_reduce: bool = USE_FP32_REDUCE_DEFAULT,
96	) -> torch.Tensor:
97	    """通过 Marlin kernel 执行 FP4 量化 linear（非 Blackwell fallback）。"""
98	    reshaped_x = input.reshape(-1, input.shape[-1])
99	    out_shape = input.shape[:-1] + (size_n,)
100	
101	    use_atomic_add = should_use_atomic_add_reduce(
102	        m=reshaped_x.size(0),
103	        n=size_n,
104	        k=size_k,
105	        device=input.device,
106	        dtype=input.dtype,
107	    )
108	
109	    output = gptq_marlin_gemm(
110	        a=reshaped_x,
111	        c=None,
112	        b_q_weight=weight,
113	        b_scales=weight_scale,
114	        global_scale=weight_global_scale.reshape(-1),
115	        b_zeros=None,
116	        g_idx=None,
117	        perm=None,
118	        workspace=workspace,
119	        b_q_type=scalar_types.float4_e2m1f,
120	        size_m=reshaped_x.size(0),
121	        size_n=size_n,
122	        size_k=size_k,
123	        use_atomic_add=use_atomic_add,
124	        use_fp32_reduce=use_fp32_reduce,
125	    )
126	
127	    if bias is not None:
128	        output.add_(bias)
129	
130	    return output.reshape(out_shape)
131	
132	
133	def prepare_fp4_layer_for_marlin(
134	    layer: torch.nn.Module,
135	    weight_attr: str = "weight",
136	    weight_scale_attr: str = "weight_scale",
137	    weight_global_scale_attr: str = "weight_global_scale",
138	) -> None:
139	    """将 NVFP4 linear 层权重原地重打包为 Marlin 格式。"""
140	    logger.warning_once(
141	        "当前 GPU 不原生支持 FP4 计算，使用 Marlin kernel 做 FP4 weight-only 推理。"
142	    )
143	
144	    part_size_n = layer.output_size_per_partition
145	    part_size_k = layer.input_size_per_partition
146	    param_dtype = layer.params_dtype
147	
148	    weight = getattr(layer, weight_attr)
149	    assert weight.shape == (part_size_n, part_size_k // 2), (
150	        f"期望 {weight_attr} shape ({part_size_n}, {part_size_k // 2})，"
151	        f"实际 {weight.shape}"
152	    )
153	
154	    device = weight.device
155	    layer.marlin_workspace = marlin_make_workspace(device)
156	
157	    # 权重重打包：NVFP4 原始布局 → Marlin tile 布局
158	    perm = torch.empty(0, dtype=torch.int, device=device)
159	    qweight = weight.data.view(torch.int32).T.contiguous()
160	    del weight
161	    marlin_qweight = gptq_marlin_repack(
162	        b_q_weight=qweight,
163	        perm=perm,
164	        size_k=part_size_k,
165	        size_n=part_size_n,
166	        num_bits=4,
167	    )
168	    del qweight
169	    setattr(layer, weight_attr, torch.nn.Parameter(marlin_qweight, requires_grad=False))
170	
171	    # scale 转换：转置 → permute → FP8-S0E5M3
172	    weight_scale = getattr(layer, weight_scale_attr)
173	    weight_scale = weight_scale.data.T.contiguous().to(param_dtype)
174	    weight_scale = marlin_permute_scales(
175	        s=weight_scale,
176	        size_k=part_size_k,
177	        size_n=part_size_n,
178	        group_size=FP4_MARLIN_GROUP_SIZE,
179	    )
180	    weight_scale = nvfp4_marlin_process_scales(weight_scale)
181	    setattr(layer, weight_scale_attr, torch.nn.Parameter(weight_scale, requires_grad=False))
182	
183	    # global scale：调整 exponent bias
184	    weight_global_scale = getattr(layer, weight_global_scale_attr)
185	    weight_global_scale = weight_global_scale.to(param_dtype)
186	    weight_global_scale = nvfp4_marlin_process_global_scale(weight_global_scale)
187	    setattr(layer, weight_global_scale_attr, torch.nn.Parameter(weight_global_scale, requires_grad=False))
188	
189	    # bias（如有）
190	    if hasattr(layer, "bias") and layer.bias is not None:
191	        assert layer.bias.shape == (part_size_n,)
192	        layer.bias = torch.nn.Parameter(marlin_permute_bias(layer.bias), requires_grad=False)

time.perf_counter()\n    for _ in range(iters):\n        fn()\n    torch.cuda.synchronize()\n    return (time.perf_counter() - t0) / iters * 1e6\n\ndef prepare_marlin_weights(K, N):\n    \"\"\"Create perm = torch.empty(0, dtype=torch.int, device=device)\n    qweight = w_nvfp4.view(torch.int32).T.contiguous()\n    marlin_qw = gptq_marlin_repack(qweight, perm, K, N, 4)\n    # Scales: (K//16, N) -> FP4_MARLIN_GROUP_SIZE)\n    marlin_scale = nvfp4_marlin_process_scales(marlin_scale)\n    # Global scale\n    gs = torch.tensor([0.01], device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n    marlin_gs = nvfp4_marlin_process_global_scale(gs)\n    workspace = marlin_make_workspace(device)\n    return marlin_qw, marlin_scale, marlin_gs, workspace\n\nhdr = \"%20s  %10s  %10s  %10s  %s\" % (\"Shape\", \"Marlin\", \"cuDNN\", \"CUTLASS\", \"Best\")\nprint(hdr)\nprint(\"-\" * len(hdr))\n\nfor M, K, N, desc in shapes:\n    results = {}\n\n    # --- Marlin (W4A16) ---\n    try:\n        marlin_qw, marlin_sc, marlin_gs, workspace = prepare_marlin_weights(K, N)\n        x_bf16 = torch.randn(M, K, device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n        def run_marlin():\n            return gptq_marlin_gemm(\n                a=x_bf16, c=None,\n                b_q_weight=marlin_qw, b_scales=marlin_sc,\n                global_scale=marlin_gs.reshape(-1),\n                b_zeros=None, g_idx=None, perm=None,\n                workspace=workspace,\n                b_q_type=scalar_types.float4_e2m1f,\n                size_m=M, size_n=N, size_k=K,\n                use_atomic_add=False, use_fp32_reduce=True,\n            )\n        results[\"marlin\"] = bench(run_marlin)\n    except Exception as e:\n        results[\"marlin\"] = None\n\n    # --- CUTLASS / cuDNN (W4A4) ---\n    a_bf16 = torch.randn(M, K, device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n    b_bf16 = torch.randn(N, K, device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n    a_gsf = (448*6) / a_bf16.float().abs().nan_to_num().max()\n    b_gsf = (448*6) / b_bf16.float().abs().nan_to_num().max()\n    aq, asc = flashinfer.nvfp4_quantize(a_bf16, a_gsf)\n    bq, bsc = flashinfer.nvfp4_quantize(b_bf16, b_gsf)\n    alpha = (1.0/(a_gsf * b_gsf)).clone().detach().to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)\n    bqt = bq.t()\n    bsct = bsc.t()\n\n    for backend in (\"cudnn\", \"cutlass\"):\n        try:\n            def run_fi(be=backend):\n                return flashinfer.mm_fp4(aq, bqt, asc, bsct, alpha, backend=be)\n            results[backend] = bench(run_fi)\n        except Exception as e:\n            results[backend] = None\n\n    # Format\n    vals = []\n    for k in (\"marlin\", \"cudnn\", \"cutlass\"):\n        v = results.get(k)\n        vals.append(v)\n\n    best_val = min((v for v in vals if v is not None), default=None)\n    strs = []\n    for v in vals:\n        if v is None:\n            strs.append(\"%10s\" % \"FAIL\")\n        else:\n            strs.append(\"%9.1fus\" % v)\n    best_name = \"\"\n    if best_val is not None:\n        names = [\"Marlin\", \"cuDNN\", \"CUTLASS\"]\n        for i, v in enumerate(vals):\n            if v == best_val:\n                best_name = names[i]\n                break\n\n    print(\"%20s  %s  %s  %s  %s\" % (desc, strs[0], strs[1], strs[2], best_name))\n\n    del results\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\nPYEOF",
  "description": "3-way benchmark: Marlin vs cuDNN vs CUTLASS",
  "timeout": 600000
}

> AGENT

所有信息齐了。总结：

---

## 3-Way Benchmark 结果

```
               Shape      Marlin       cuDNN     CUTLASS  Best
--------------------------------------------------------------
            M=1 qkvo       10.4us       73.7us       35.5us  Marlin
         M=1 gate/up       16.5us       71.4us       44.7us  Marlin
            M=1 down       20.6us       73.5us      160.1us  Marlin
         M=4 gate/up       16.5us       72.8us       47.5us  Marlin
            M=4 down       19.3us       71.6us      160.7us  Marlin
         M=8 gate/up       16.7us       68.9us       45.0us  Marlin
            M=8 down       20.6us      144.2us      143.5us  Marlin
        M=16 gate/up       23.1us       70.5us       43.3us  Marlin
           M=16 down       20.6us       70.4us      160.4us  Marlin
        M=32 gate/up       26.6us       67.3us       40.2us  Marlin
           M=32 down       39.0us       70.5us      160.8us  Marlin
        M=48 gate/up       42.1us       67.5us       36.5us  CUTLASS
           M=48 down       49.9us       68.5us      158.1us  Marlin
        M=64 gate/up       58.0us       66.9us       40.6us  CUTLASS
           M=64 down       61.6us       69.1us      158.4us  Marlin
       M=128 gate/up      127.1us       66.6us       38.1us  CUTLASS
          M=128 down      121.8us       69.2us      160.9us  cuDNN
       M=256 gate/up      252.3us       77.0us       60.1us  CUTLASS
       M=512 gate/up      504.5us      136.3us      140.0us  cuDNN
      M=1024 gate/up     1001.7us      259.9us      264.2us  cuDNN
      M=2048 gate/up     2007.6us      528.1us      516.7us  CUTLASS
      M=4096 gate/up     4021.2us     1029.5us     1010.9us  CUTLASS
      M=8192 gate/up     8044.6us     1997.7us     1978.8us  CUTLASS
```

### 关键结论

**1. Marlin (W4A16) 在小 M decode 是碾压级的**

M≤32 时全场景最快，领先 CUTLASS 1.5-8x，领先 cuDNN 3-8x。M=48 时 down_proj 仍然最快。Marlin 本质是 GEMV，O(M×N) 而非 O(M×N×K)，小 M 下天然优势。

**2. Marlin 的 crossover 在 M=48 附近**

- gate/up (K=4096→N=16384)：M=48 时 Marlin 42us vs CUTLASS 36us，CUTLASS 反超
- down (K=16384→N=4096)：M=64 时 Marlin 62us vs cuDNN 69us，仍有微优

**3. cuDNN 几乎没有明确优势区间**

- down_proj M=128 赢 CUTLASS (69 vs 161us)，但 Marlin 也只要 122us
- gate/up M=512-1024 微赢 CUTLASS，但差距 <3%
- 总体上 cuDNN 有 ~65us 的固定开销，小 M 时全输

**4. CUTLASS 在 M≥48 的 gate/up 最优**

gate/up_proj (宽矩阵 N=16384) 从 M=48 起 CUTLASS 稳定最快。

### 最优混合策略

当前 Marlin hybrid (threshold=48) 已经接近最优：

| M 范围 | 最优 backend | 当前方案 | 差距 |
|--------|-------------|---------|------|
| M ≤ 32 | **Marlin** | Marlin ✅ | 0 |
| M = 48 | gate/up CUTLASS, down Marlin | Marlin ✅ (可微优) | 极小 |
| M ≥ 64 | gate/up CUTLASS, down cuDNN | CUTLASS | **down_proj 2.3x** |
| M ≥ 128 | gate/up CUTLASS, down cuDNN | CUTLASS | **down_proj 2.3x** |

**cuDNN 唯一有价值的场景是 M≥64 时的 down_proj**，能比 CUTLASS 快 2.3x (69us vs 160us)。但在实际 serving 中这个 M 区间并发 decode 频率不高（S1 始终 M=1，S8 最多 M=8，Smax 才会到 M=64）。

### NVFP4 可用的全部加速后端

| Backend | SM 要求 | 本项目可用 | 说明 |
|---------|--------|-----------|------|
| **CUTLASS** (FlashInfer JIT) | SM100/103/110/120/121 | ✅ 当前默认 | W4A4, JIT 编译 |
| **cuDNN** (graph API) | SM100/103/110/120/121 | ✅ 刚解锁 | W4A4, 需 cuDNN ≥9.13 |
| **TRT-LLM** | **SM100/103 only** | ❌ SM120 不支持 | W4A4, low-latency kernel |
| **CuTe-DSL** | **SM100/103 only** | ❌ SM120 不支持 | W4A4, CUTLASS DSL, PDL |
| **Marlin** (sgl-kernel) | SM ≥ 75 | ✅ 已有 | W4A16, GEMV, decode 专用 |

**结论：SM120 上能用的 NVFP4 后端就只有 CUTLASS、cuDNN、Marlin 三个。TRT-LLM 和 CuTe-DSL 都锁定 SM100/103 (Blackwell B200/B300)，没有 SM120 支持。没有更多可选了。**

Based on the search results, here's what I found regarding TensorRT-LLM SM120 support for RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell:

## Current Support Status

The trtllm-gen FMHA (Fused Multi-Head Attention) kernels used by flashinfer lack pre-compiled cubins for SM120 (RTX PRO 6000) and SM121, which blocks the trtllm-gen FMHA backend on all consumer Blackwell GPUs. This was reported as a feature request on February 28, 2026.

However, there has been progress on other fronts. FP8 support was added for SM120 architecture, and TensorRT-LLM expanded Blackwell/Hopper/Ampere enablement including B300/GB200/GB300 and SM120/SM121/SM103 paths.

## Known Limitations

RTX Pro 6000 Blackwell is not equivalent to datacenter Blackwell for inference. The SM120 design lacks the hardware needed for FlashAttention-4, and that directly limits performance improvements in modern inference stacks.

There are also ongoing challenges with certain features on SM120, such as issues with MoE (Mixture of Experts) kernels that were originally compiled for SM100 hardware not properly initializing on SM120.

In summary, while TensorRT-LLM has added expanding support for SM120, full feature parity with datacenter Blackwell (SM100) remains a work in progress as of 2026.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

Based on the search results, here's what I found regarding TensorRT-LLM FP4 GEMM support for SM120 and SM121 compute capabilities:

## Key Findings

### SM121 (GB10/Blackwell Consumer) Issues

The FP4 CUTLASS GEMM (nvfp4_gemm_cutlass) fails on GB10 (SM121) because the code routes SM121 to the SM120 path, but both SM120 tile configurations require more shared memory than GB10 provides. GB10 only has 99 KiB of shared memory versus B200's ~228 KiB.

The alternative implementation nvfp4_gemm_cublaslt works fine on the same hardware, indicating this is a kernel-specific issue rather than a fundamental hardware limitation.

### Recent Fixes

A fix for "FP4 CUTLASS GEMM shared memory overflow on GB10 (SM121)" was recently implemented, addressing this compatibility issue.

### SM120 (RTX PRO 6000) and Overall Support

TensorRT-LLM 1.0 added support for SM121. FP8 support for SM120 architecture was also added, though the search results focus more heavily on SM121 issues related to FP4 GEMM specifically.

The main challenge has been ensuring SM120/SM121 kernels have appropriate tile configurations and shared memory constraints suited to consumer Blackwell GPUs rather than data-center variants.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

I've found relevant information about NVIDIA TRT-LLM and SM120 support. Here are the key findings from the search results:

## SM120 Support & Features

FP8 support for SM120 architecture has been added to TensorRT-LLM. Additionally, expanded Blackwell/Hopper/Ampere enablement includes SM120/SM121/SM103 paths.

## Low Latency GEMM

The GEMM plugin is optimized specially for low latency scenarios, and there is a GEMM + SwiGLU fusion plugin that optimized specially for low latency scenarios.

## Recent Fixes & Optimizations

The search results show several relevant fixes related to SM120:
- A fix for MoE regression on SM120 and issues with illegal memory access in MoE GEMM on SM120 were addressed
- Fixes for MoE FP4 on SM120 have been implemented

## Additional Context

CuteDSL integrated NVFP4 grouped GEMM for Blackwell, and low-precision capabilities have been broadened including FP8/NVFP4/MXFP4/INT4-AWQ.

You can find more detailed information in the official NVIDIA TensorRT-LLM GitHub repository at https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM, which contains release notes, issues, and documentation about the roadmap and features.

Based on the search results, I found several relevant GitHub issues related to FlashInfer, TRT-LLM, low-latency GEMM, and SM120 support:

## Key Issues Found:

**1. FlashInfer Issue #2577 - NVFP4 mm_fp4 GEMM broken on SM120**
All NVFP4 mm_fp4 GEMM backends fail on SM120 (RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell), with the trtllm backend specifically returning a BackendSupportedError indicating that mm_fp4 does not support backend 'trtllm' with capability 120.

**2. FlashInfer Issue #2555 - SM120 attention kernels blocked by wiring issues**
SM120 attention kernels already exist in the codebase but are not wired into the runtime pipeline.

**3. TensorRT-LLM Issue #11799 - Missing SM120/SM121 FMHA cubins**
The trtllm-gen FMHA kernels used by flashinfer lack pre-compiled cubins for SM120 (RTX PRO 6000) and SM121, which blocks the trtllm-gen FMHA backend on all consumer Blackwell GPUs.

**4. FlashInfer Issue #2723 - NVFP4 MoE grouped GEMM failures on SM120**
CUTLASS and FlashInfer NVFP4 MoE grouped GEMM fails on SM120 desktop Blackwell GPUs, with workarounds requiring systematic patching of SM120 capability checks and CuTe DSL architecture restrictions.

These issues represent ongoing efforts to add comprehensive SM120 support for low-latency GEMM operations in FlashInfer and related projects.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

{"body":"## Feature Description\n\nThe trtllm-gen FMHA (Fused Multi-Head Attention) kernels used by flashinfer lack pre-compiled cubins for SM120 (RTX PRO 6000) and SM121 (DGX Spark GB10, RTX 5090). This blocks the trtllm-gen FMHA backend on all consumer Blackwell GPUs.\n\n## Background\n\nflashinfer uses pre-compiled FMHA cubins sourced from TensorRT-LLM. These cubins are published as artifacts and downloaded at JIT compilation time ([`flashinfer/artifacts.py`](https://github.com/flashinfer-ai/flashinfer/blob/main/flashinfer/artifacts.py)). The cubin metadata header (`flashInferMetaInfo.h`) encodes which architectures each kernel supports, and the loader matches the GPU's SM version against available kernels via `isSMCompatible()`.\n\nCurrently, only SM100 and SM103 cubins exist in the artifact repository. The SM120/SM121 constants are already defined in flashinfer's [`common.h`](https://github.com/flashinfer-ai/flashinfer/blob/main/include/flashinfer/trtllm/common.h#L329-L330) but are unused.\n\n## Why this cannot be resolved downstream\n\nThere are two blockers, both requiring upstream TensorRT-LLM changes:\n\n1. **No SM120/SM121 cubins in the artifact repository.** flashinfer downloads pre-compiled cubins — it does not compile FMHA kernels from source. Without SM12x cubins published by TensorRT-LLM, there are no kernels to load.\n\n2. **SM120's ISA differs from SM100.** SM120 (consumer Blackwell) does not have `tcgen05` instructions or TMEM — it uses `mma.sync.aligned.block_scale` instead of `tcgen05.mma`. The SM100 FMHA kernel source cannot simply be recompiled for SM120; it requires architectural adaptation for SM120's instruction set.\n\nOn the flashinfer side, there is also a hardcoded arch check in [`fmhaRunner.cuh:30`](https://github.com/flashinfer-ai/flashinfer/blob/main/include/flashinfer/trtllm/fmha/fmhaRunner.cuh#L30):\n\n```cpp\nFLASHINFER_CHECK(mSM == kSM_100 || mSM == kSM_103, \"Unsupported architecture\");\n```\n\nThis can be relaxed on the flashinfer side once SM12x cubins become available, but without the cubins it's a moot point.\n\n## Related Issues and PRs\n\n- flashinfer [#2555](https://github.com/flashinfer-ai/flashinfer/issues/2555) — SM120 attention kernels blocked by wiring issues (tracks the flashinfer-side arch guard)\n- flashinfer [#2560](https://github.com/flashinfer-ai/flashinfer/pull/2560) — Guards CUTLASS FMHA against SM12x (approved, pending merge)\n- TensorRT-LLM [#11386](https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/issues/11386) — SM120 dependency regression in 1.3.0rc2\n- TensorRT-LLM [#10241](https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/issues/10241) — NVFP4 KV cache support for SM120 in trtllm-gen\n\n## Expected Behavior\n\nSM120/SM121 devices should be able to use the trtllm-gen FMHA backend, either via:\n- SM12x-native FMHA cubins compiled with SM120-compatible instructions, or\n- Fallback cubins compatible with SM12x (if SM100f cubins can run on SM12x hardware)\n\n## Environment\n\n- Hardware: NVIDIA GB10 (DGX Spark, SM121a)\n- flashinfer: 0.6.x (main branch)\n- CUDA: 13.0\n\nContributed by Second Nature Computing this, which has the same blocker:\n\nThe FlashInfer MoE FP4 batched GEMM kernels (used by vLLM via `VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_MOE_FP4=1`) also rely on pre-compiled cubins sourced from the NVIDIA artifactory:\n\n```\nhttps://edge.urm.nvidia.com/artifactory/sw-kernelinferencelibrary-public-generic-local/\n456b1ae890d436c794b17e4435b41b849d3e5950/batched_gemm-2a674db-3a84a12/\n```\n\nThe cubin Title: SM120 Performance Optimization Plan
State: OPEN
Created: 2026-03-02T02:00:33Z
Updated: 2026-04-02T06:17:22Z

## Motivation

Recently, the community has been looking for improved SM120 support, so we'd like to improve the performance and usability across various models.

# Models
Some popular models requested:

DSA (not currently supported):
- `deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V3.2`, `nvidia/DeepSeek-V3.2-NVFP4`
- `zai-org/GLM-5-FP8`

MLA:
- `nvidia/DeepSeek-V3.1-NVFP4`
- `nvidia/Qwen3.5-397B-A17B-NVFP4`
- Use `fmha_v2_prefill_deepseek` for MLA prefill
- Use XQA for MLA decode

GDN:
- `nvidia/Qwen3.5-397B-A17B-NVFP4`

MHA:
- `nvidia/Qwen3.5-397B-A17B-NVFP4`
- `baseten-admin/glm-4.7-fp4`

## Attention & Heuristics
- Enable XQA for MHA optimization #17115
- Re-enable Flashinfer for full attention in hybrid models https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/20604
- Add some better heuristics for attention backend selection (`fa2, xqa, cudnn`)
- Use CuDNN prefill and decode through Flashinfer API `cudnn` decode also cannot be selected through the attention wrapper so it may need seperate integration
- Support Spec V2 (overlap scheduling + speculative decoding) in Flashinfer attention backend (currently, it has a sync from `plan_stream`). Currently, only `xqa` MHA can support this for SM120.

## Mixed Precision & Core Types (NVFP4 / MXFP8 / MXFP4)
- NVFP4 KV Cache #18314
- MXFP8 functional enablement #19112 (Done)
- MXFP4 functional enablement with `triton_kernels` #19718 #20040

## Backend & Kernel Optimization (CUTLASS/cuDNN)
- Support BF16 x BF16 GEMM from Cute-DSL example in CUTLASS repo, as currently, `torch.linear` will generate a kernel for Ampere architecture (SM80) (confirmed can be fixed by upgrading Torch)
- FP8 blockscale CUTLASS backend to replace Triton blockscale #18872 #20887
- Support MXFP8 grouped GEMM through CUTLASS
- Support MXFP8 dense GEMM through CUTLASS
- Support/Improve per-tensor/per-channel FP8 through CUTLASS
- Support MXFP4 x MXFP4 (furinvestigate/benchmark if the current one is through cuDNN, or CUTLASS)
- Improve NVFP4 x NVFP4 (currently, cuDNN outperforms CUTLASS implementation in all scenarios, even though it has StreamK) #20047 https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/21314 Further improvement can utilize SwapAB in conjunction with mixed TMA recipe, perhaps both for dense and MoE GEMM.
### MoE specific
- Improve `--moe-runner-backend=flashinfer_cutlass` (`cutlass` is just a port of the `flashinfer_cutlass` version): improve various small kernels overhead, support (if not already support) fuse shared to routed experts, improve grouped GEMM (highest importance) on SM120. It supports BF16, FP8, and NVFP4, so ideally **all** of these can be improved. Some users have reported `cutlass` is better than `flashinfer_cutlass`, we should investigate why. With some improvements, it should be able to beat `triton` in all scenarios.

## Communication
- Support TRTLLM allreduce fusion and benchmark if it is faster, SM120 for 6KD can support P2P communication through PCIe, but it doesn't support the feature of multicast. After an initial attempt, it i
```

Updated: 2026-03-10T04:56:36Z

|-----------|---------|
| GPU | NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition (8x) |
| Compute Capability | SM 12.0 |
| CUDA | 13 |
| Driver | 580.126.09 |
| FlashInfer | 0.6.3 |
| PyTorch | 2.9.1 |
| OS | Ubuntu 24.04 |

## Bug Description

All NVFP4 `mm_fp4` GEMM backends fail on SM120 (RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell):

| Backend | Result |
|---------|--------|
| `cudnn` | `cudnnGraphNotSupportedError: No execution plans support the graph` |
| `cutlass` | Returns **all zeros** (silent failure) |
| `trtllm` | `BackendSupportedError: mm_fp4 does not support backend 'trtllm' with capability 120` |

Note: SM120 is the **consumer/prosumer Blackwell** architecture (RTX 5090, RTX PRO 6000), distinct from SM100 (B200/B300 datacenter Blackwell).

## Reproduction
```python
import torch
from flashinfer import nvfp4_quantize, mm_fp4

print('GPU:', torch.cuda.get_device_name())
print('Compute capability:', torch.cuda.get_device_capability())

# Create test tensors
m, k, n = 128, 256, 512
a = torch.randn(m, k, device='cuda', dtype=torch.bfloat16) * 10
b = torch.randn(n, k, device='cuda', dtype=torch.bfloat16) * 10

# Reference
ref = torch.matmul(a, b.T)
print(f'Reference range: [{ref.min().item():.1f}, {ref.max().item():.1f}]')

# Quantize
a_scale = (448.0 * 6.0) / a.abs().max()
b_scale = (448.0 * 6.0) / b.abs().max()
a_fp4, a_sf = nvfp4_quantize(a, a_scale)
b_fp4, b_sf = nvfp4_quantize(b, b_scale)
alpha = torch.tensor(1.0 / (a_scale.item() * b_scale.item()), device='cuda', dtype=torch.float32)

# Test each backend
for backend in ['cutlass', 'cudnn', 'trtllm']:
    try:
        result = mm_fp4(a_fp4, b_fp4.T, a_sf, b_sf.T, alpha, torch.bfloat16, backend=backend)
        all_zero = (result == 0).all().item()
        has_nan = torch.isnan(result).any().item()
        print(f'{backend}: range [{result.min().item():.1f}, {result.max().item():.1f}], zeros={all_zero}, nan={has_nan}')
    except Exception as e:
        print(f'{backend}: FAILED - {type(e

信息已经足够了。总结：

---

## TRT-LLM 对 SM120 的支持现状

### 核心结论：**短期内不会有 SM120 的 TRT-LLM 低延迟 kernel**

### 三个关键 Issue 的官方回复

| Issue | 内容 | 官方态度 |
|-------|------|---------|
| [NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#10241](https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/issues/10241) | SM120 NVFP4 KV cache | NVIDIA 官方 (laikhtewari): **"TensorRT LLM isn't designed to work on RTX cards — we focus on datacenter deployment"**。后被纠正 RTX PRO 6000 SE 是数据中心卡后改口：**"Will add this feature request to roadmap"** (2026-01-22)。至今未关闭 |
| [NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#11799](https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/issues/11799) | SM120/121 FMHA cubins | NVIDIA 官方 (pengbowang-nv): **"trtllm-gen does not have support for SM120/121 yet. And SM100 kernels cannot run on SM120 directly."** (2026-03-18)。建议用 XQA 或 FMHA_v2 替代 |
| [NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM#7484](https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/issues/7484) | SM120 FP4 MOE | 仅标记为 question，无实质回复 (2025-09) |

### 根本原因：SM120 和 SM100 ISA 不兼容

SM120（消费级/专业级 Blackwell）和 SM100（数据中心 Blackwell B200/B300）在指令集层面有本质区别：

- **SM100**: 有 `tcgen05` 指令 + TMEM（Tensor Memory）
- **SM120**: **没有** `tcgen05`，使用 `mma.sync.aligned.block_scale` 替代

这意味着 SM100 的 FMHA cubin、FP4 batched GEMM cubin **不能直接在 SM120 上运行**。需要针对 SM120 重新编写 kernel，不是简单重编译。

### FlashInfer 的 `trtllm` backend 为什么锁 SM100/103

FlashInfer 的 `mm_fp4(backend="trtllm")` 调用的是从 NVIDIA artifactory 下载的预编译 cubin。这些 cubin：
- 只有 SM100 版本
- SM120 在 `@supported_compute_capability([100, 103])` 装饰器中被直接排除
- 同样 `cute-dsl` backend 也锁 `[100, 103]`

### SGLang SM120 优化计划 ([sgl-project/sglang#19637](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/issues/19637))

SGLang 有一个 SM120 性能优化跟踪 issue (2026-03-02 创建)，其中提到：

- **cuDNN 在 SM120 上 NVFP4 GEMM 全场景优于 CUTLASS**（甚至有 StreamK 的情况下）
- 计划通过 cuDNN SwapAB + mixed TMA 进一步优化
- 注意力方面走 XQA/FMHA_v2/cuDNN，不依赖 trtllm-gen

### 对我们项目的意义

**TRT-LLM 的 FP4 低延迟 kernel（trtllm、cute-dsl）在可预见的未来不会支持 SM120。** SM120 上 NVFP4 的可用 backend 就是刚才测的三个：

| Backend | 状态 | 前景 |
|---------|------|------|
| **Marlin** (W4A16) | ✅ 已有，decode 最优 | 维持现状 |
| **CUTLASS** (W4A4) | ✅ 已有，prefill 最优 | FlashInfer 锁定 0.6.7.post3，无更新 |
| **cuDNN** (W4A4) | ✅ 刚解锁 | SGLang 官方方向，down_proj 有优势 |
| trtllm | ❌ SM100 only | **无计划支持 SM120** |
| cute-dsl | ❌ SM100/103 only | **无计划支持 SM120** |

**我们当前的 Marlin+CUTLASS hybrid 方案已经是 SM120 上接近最优的选择。** cuDNN 在 down_proj 上的 2-3x 优势可以考虑做 per-projection 混合，但总体提升有限（只影响 M≥64 的 down_proj）。

Sources:
- [TRT-LLM #11799 - Compile trtllm-gen FMHA cubins for SM120/SM121](https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/issues/11799)
- [TRT-LLM #10241 - Add NVFP4 KV cache support for SM120 in trtllm-gen](https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/issues/10241)
- [TRT-LLM #7484 - Only SM100 is supported by FP4 block scale MOE](https://github.com/NVIDIA/TensorRT-LLM/issues/7484)
- [FlashInfer #2577 - NVFP4 mm_fp4 GEMM broken on SM120](https://github.com/flashinfer-ai/flashinfer/issues/2577)
- [SGLang #19637 - SM120 Performance Optimization Plan](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/issues/19637)

379	    def forward(
380	        self,
381	        input_ids,
382	        hidden_states,
383	        lm_head: VocabParallelEmbedding,
384	        logits_metadata: Union[LogitsMetadata, ForwardBatch],
385	        aux_hidden_states: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
386	        hidden_states_before_norm: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,
387	    ) -> LogitsProcessorOutput:
388	        if isinstance(logits_metadata, ForwardBatch):
389	            logits_metadata = LogitsMetadata.from_forward_batch(logits_metadata)
390	
391	        # Check if multi-item scoring is enabled via server args (only for prefill-only requests)
392	        multi_item_delimiter = get_global_server_args().multi_item_scoring_delimiter
393	        if multi_item_delimiter is not None and logits_metadata.is_prefill_only:
394	            return self.compute_logprobs_for_multi_item_scoring(
395	                input_ids, hidden_states, lm_head, logits_metadata, multi_item_delimiter
396	            )
397	
398	        if logits_metadata.forward_mode.is_dllm_extend():
399	            assert self.return_full_logits
400	            full_logits = self._get_logits(hidden_states, lm_head, logits_metadata)
401	            return LogitsProcessorOutput(
402	                full_logits=full_logits,
403	                next_token_logits=None,
404	            )
405	
406	        # Get the last hidden states and last logits for the next token prediction
407	        pruned_states_before_norm: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None
408	        if (
409	            logits_metadata.forward_mode.is_decode_or_idle()
410	            or logits_metadata.forward_mode.is_target_verify()
411	            or logits_metadata.forward_mode.is_draft_extend_v2()
412	        ):
413	            pruned_states = hidden_states
414	            pruned_states_before_norm = hidden_states_before_norm
415	            if aux_hidden_states is not None:
416	                aux_pruned_states = [hidden for hidden in aux_hidden_states]
417	            sample_indices = None
418	            input_logprob_indices = None
419	
420	        elif (
421	            logits_metadata.forward_mode.is_extend()
422	            and not logits_metadata.extend_return_logprob
423	        ):
424	            # Prefill without input logprobs.
425	            if logits_metadata.padded_static_len < 0:
426	                last_index = torch.cumsum(logits_metadata.extend_seq_lens, dim=0) - 1
427	            else:
428	                # If padding_static length is 5 and extended_seq_lens is [2, 3],
429	                # then our batch looks like [t00, t01, p, p, p, t10, t11, t12, p, p]
430	                # and this retrieves t01 and t12, which are the valid last tokens
431	                idx = torch.arange(
432	                    len(logits_metadata.extend_seq_lens),
433	                    device=logits_metadata.extend_seq_lens.device,
434	                )
435	                last_index = (
436	                    idx * logits_metadata.padded_static_len
437	                    + logits_metadata.extend_seq_lens
438	                    - 1
439	                )
440	            pruned_states = hidden_states[last_index]
441	            if hidden_states_before_norm is not None:
442	                pruned_states_before_norm = hidden_states_before_norm[last_index]
443	            if aux_hidden_states is not None:
444	                aux_pruned_states = [hidden[last_index] for hidden in aux_hidden_states]
445	            sample_indices = None
446	            input_logprob_indices = None
447	        else:
448	            # Prefill with input logprobs.
449	            # Find 4 different indices.
450	            # 1. pruned_states: hidden states that we want logprobs from.
451	            # 2. sample_indices: Indices that have sampled tokens.
452	            # 3. input_logprob_indices: Indices that have input logprob tokens.
453	            # 4. token_to_seq_idx: map each token to its sequence index
454	            #
455	            # Example
456	            # -------
457	            # Suppose a batch (flattened by sequence):
458	            # [t00, t01, t02, t03, t10, t11, t12, t13, t14, t20, t21, t22, t23, t24, t25]

49	        try:
50	            mod = __import__(mod_name)
51	            lines.append(f"{mod_name}: {getattr(mod, '__version__', 'unknown')}")
52	            lines.append(f"{mod_name}_path: {getattr(mod, '__file__', 'unknown')}")
53	        except Exception as e:
54	            lines.append(f"{mod_name}: error {e}")
55	
56	    try:
57	        import sgl_kernel
58	        lines.append(f"sgl_kernel: {sgl_kernel.__version__}")
59	        lines.append(f"common_ops: {sgl_kernel.common_ops.__file__}")
60	    except Exception as e:
61	        lines.append(f"sgl_kernel: error {e}")
62	
63	    lines.append(f"SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD: {os.environ.get('SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD', 'unset')}")
64	    lines.append(f"SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD: {os.environ.get('SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD', 'unset')}")
65	    lines.append(f"CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG: {os.environ.get('CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG', 'unset')}")
66	    lines.append(f"SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS: {os.environ.get('SGLANG_SERVER_ARGS', 'unset')}")
67	    return "\n".join(lines)
68	
69	
70	def extract_final_answer(pred: str) -> str:
71	    parts = pred.split("</think>")
72	    return parts[-1].strip() if len(parts) > 1 else pred
73	
74	
75	def extract_mcq_answer(pred: str):
76	    m = re.search(r"(?i)ANSWER\s*:\s*([A-D])", pred)
77	    if m:
78	        return m.group(1).upper()
79	    m = re.search(r"\\boxed\{\\text\{([A-D])\}\}", pred)
80	    if m:
81	        return m.group(1).upper()
82	    m = re.search(r"\\boxed\{([A-D])\}", pred)
83	    if m:
84	        return m.group(1).upper()
85	    return None
86	
87	
88	def score_item(pred: str, item: dict) -> tuple[float, str | None]:
89	    task = item.get("task", "unknown")
90	    gold = item.get("gold")
91	    if not pred or not gold:
92	        return 0.0, None
93	
94	    final = extract_final_answer(pred)
95	
96	    if task == "mcq":
97	        extracted = extract_mcq_answer(final)
98	        return (1.0 if extracted and extracted.upper() == gold.upper() else 0.0), extracted
99	    if task in ("qa", "niah", "lcx"):
100	        golds = gold if isinstance(gold, list) else [gold]
101	        hit = any(str(g).lower() in final.lower() for g in golds)
102	        return (1.0 if hit else 0.0), None
103	    if task in ("cwe", "fwe"):
104	        golds = gold if isinstance(gold, list) else [gold]
105	        hits = sum(1.0 for g in golds if str(g).lower() in final.lower())
106	        return (hits / len(golds) if golds else 0.0), None
107	    if isinstance(gold, str) and gold.lower() in pred.lower():
108	        return 1.0, None
109	    return 0.0, None
110	
111	
112	def start_server(model_path: str, server_args: str, empty_debug_path: Path) -> subprocess.Popen:
113	    import os
114	
115	    env = os.environ.copy()
116	    env["SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD"] = "36"
117	    env.pop("SGLANG_MEDUSA_BS_THRESHOLD", None)
118	    env["CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG"] = ":4096:8"
119	    env["SGLANG_EMPTY_RESPONSE_DEBUG_PATH"] = str(empty_debug_path)
120	    cmd = (
121	        f"python3 -m sglang.launch_server "
122	        f"--model-path {model_path} "
123	        f"--trust-remote-code --port {SERVER_PORT} "
124	        f"{server_args}"
125	    )
126	    print(f"[probe] starting server: {cmd}", flush=True)
127	    print(f"[probe] env: MARLIN_THRESHOLD=36 MEDUSA_BS=disabled CUBLAS={env['CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG']}", flush=True)
128	    script_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
129	    server_log = script_dir / "server_log.txt"
130	    log_fh = open(server_log, "w")
131	    proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, shell=True, stdout=log_fh, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, env=env)
132	    proc._log_fh = log_fh
133	    proc._log_path = server_log
134	    return proc
135	
136	
137	def wait_for_server(wait_seconds: int = FIXED_STARTUP_WAIT_SEC) -> None:
138	    print(f"[probe] waiting fixed {wait_seconds}s before eval (no polling)...", flush=True)
139	    time.sleep(wait_seconds)
140	    print("[probe] fixed wait complete, proceeding to eval", flush=True)
141	
142	
143	def kill_server(proc: subprocess.Popen):
144	    proc.terminate()
145	    try:
146	        proc.wait(timeout=10)
147	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
148	        proc.kill()
149	    if hasattr(proc, "_log_fh"):
150	        proc._log_fh.close()
151	
152	
153	def call_api(model: str, prompt: str, rid: str, timeout: int = 3600) -> tuple[str, dict, str | None]:
154	    payload = {
155	        "model": model,
156	        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
157	        "temperature": 0.0,
158	        "max_tokens": MAX_TOKENS,
159	        "stop": STOP_WORDS,
160	        "chat_template_kwargs": {"enable_thinking": True},
161	        "rid": rid,
162	        "user": rid,
163	    }
164	    try:
165	        resp = requests.post(f"{API_BASE}/v1/chat/completions", json=payload, timeout=timeout)
166	        resp.raise_for_status()
167	        result = resp.json()
168	        choice = result["choices"][0]
169	        content = choice["message"].get("content") or ""
170	        usage = result.get("usage", {})
171	        finish_reason = choice.get("finish_reason")
172	        return content, usage, finish_reason
173	    except Exception as e:
174	        print(f"[probe] request error: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
175	        return "", {}, None
176	
177	
178	def read_server_log(script_dir: Path, max_lines: int | None = None) -> str:
179	    server_log_path = script_dir / "server_log.txt"
180	    if not server_log_path.exists():
181	        return "<missing server_log.txt>"
182	    lines = server_log_path.read_text(errors="replace").splitlines()
183	    if max_lines is not None:
184	        lines = lines[:max_lines]
185	    return "\n".join(lines)
186	
187	
188	def main():
189	    script_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
190	    report_dir = script_dir / "probe_reports"
191	    report_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
192	    model_path = sys.argv[1]
193	    data_path = script_dir / "data" / "perf_public_set.jsonl"
194	    empty_debug_path = script_dir / "empty_response_debug.jsonl"
195	    if empty_debug_path.exists():
196	        empty_debug_path.unlink()
197	
198	    server_args = (
199	        "--disable-radix-cache --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer "
200	        "--chunked-prefill-size 8192 --skip-server-warmup --dense-as-sparse "
201	        "--quantization modelopt_fp4 --max-running-requests 64 "
202	        "--mem-fraction-static 0.80"
203	    )
204	
205	    env_info = collect_env_info()
206	    quant_log_path = script_dir / "quant_log.txt"
207	    quant_log = quant_log_path.read_text(errors="replace") if quant_log_path.exists() else ""
208	
209	    print("[probe] starting server...", flush=True)
210	    proc = start_server(model_path, server_args, empty_debug_path)
211	    wait_for_server(FIXED_STARTUP_WAIT_SEC)
212	
213	    model_name = model_path
214	    print(f"[probe] fixed wait complete, starting eval directly with model={model_name}", flush=True)
215	
216	    dataset = []
217	    with open(data_path) as f:
218	        for line in f:
219	            if line.strip():
220	                dataset.append(json.loads(line))
221	    print(f"[probe] {len(dataset)} samples loaded", flush=True)
222	    print(f"[probe] eval_max_tokens={MAX_TOKENS}", flush=True)
223	
224	    from transformers import AutoTokenizer, GenerationConfig
225	    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)
226	
227	    global STOP_WORDS
228	    try:
229	        gc = GenerationConfig.from_pretrained(model_path)
230	        if hasattr(gc, "eos_token_id"):
231	            eos = gc.eos_token_id
232	            ids = [eos] if isinstance(eos, int) else (eos or [])
233	            for tid in ids:
234	                w = tokenizer.decode(tid)
235	                if w:
236	                    STOP_WORDS.append(w)
237	    except Exception:
238	        pass
239	    if tokenizer.eos_token:
240	        STOP_WORDS.append(tokenizer.eos_token)
241	    STOP_WORDS = list(set(s for s in STOP_WORDS if s))
242	    print(f"[probe] stop_words={STOP_WORDS}", flush=True)
243	
244	    results = [None] * len(dataset)
245	    t0 = time.time()
246	    empty_count = 0
247	
248	    def _infer(idx):
249	        rid = f"probe-{idx}"
250	        return idx, rid, *call_api(model_name, dataset[idx]["question"], rid)
251	
252	    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=CONCURRENCY) as ex:
253	        futures = {ex.submit(_infer, i): i for i in range(len(dataset))}
254	        done = 0
255	        for fut in as_completed(futures):
256	            idx, rid, content, usage, finish_reason = fut.result()
257	            item = dataset[idx]
258	            task = item.get("task", "unknown")
259	            gold = item.get("gold")
260	
261	            is_empty = (content is None or content.strip() == "")
262	            if is_empty:
263	                empty_count += 1
264	
265	            score_val, extracted = score_item(content or "", item)
266	
267	            in_tokens = usage.get("prompt_tokens", 0)
268	            out_tokens = usage.get("completion_tokens", 0)
269	            if in_tokens == 0:
270	                try:
271	                    in_tokens = len(tokenizer.encode(item["question"]))
272	                except Exception:
273	                    pass
274	            if out_tokens == 0 and content:
275	                try:
276	                    out_tokens = len(tokenizer.encode(content))
277	                except Exception:
278	                    pass
279	
280	            results[idx] = {
281	                "index": idx,
282	                "rid": rid,
283	                "task": task,
284	                "question": item["question"][:500],
285	                "gold": gold,
286	                "prediction": content or "",
287	                "score": score_val,
288	                "extracted": extracted,
289	                "input_tokens": in_tokens,
290	                "output_tokens": out_tokens,
291	                "finish_reason": finish_reason,
292	                "usage": usage,
293	                "empty": is_empty,
294	            }
295	
296	            done += 1
297	            if done % 10 == 0 or done == len(dataset):
298	                elapsed = time.time() - t0
299	                partial_score = sum(r["score"] for r in results if r is not None)
300	                partial_done = sum(1 for r in results if r is not None)
301	                partial_acc = partial_score / partial_done * 100 if partial_done else 0
302	                print(
303	                    f"[probe] {done}/{len(dataset)} acc={partial_acc:.2f}% empty={empty_count} elapsed={elapsed:.0f}s",
304	                    flush=True,
305	                )
306	
307	    elapsed = time.time() - t0
308	    kill_server(proc)
309	
310	    valid = [r for r in results if r is not None]
311	    total_score = sum(r["score"] for r in valid)
312	    ori_accuracy = (total_score / len(valid)) * 100 if valid else 0.0
313	    total_input = sum(r["input_tokens"] for r in valid)
314	    total_output = sum(r["output_tokens"] for r in valid)
315	    empty_total = sum(1 for r in valid if r["empty"])
316	
317	    task_breakdown = {}
318	    for r in valid:
319	        t = r["task"]
320	        task_breakdown.setdefault(t, {"correct": 0, "total": 0, "empty": 0})
321	        task_breakdown[t]["total"] += 1
322	        task_breakdown[t]["correct"] += r["score"]
323	        if r["empty"]:
324	            task_breakdown[t]["empty"] += 1
325	
326	    final_state = collect_state("post_eval")
327	    empty_debug_text = (
328	        empty_debug_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
329	        if empty_debug_path.exists()
330	        else ""
331	    )
332	    empty_debug_count = len([line for line in empty_debug_text.splitlines() if line.strip()])
333	    summary = {
334	        "ori_accuracy": round(ori_accuracy, 2),
335	        "overall_accuracy": min(round(ori_accuracy / 80 * 100, 2), 100),
336	        "num_samples": len(valid),
337	        "empty_responses": empty_total,
338	        "duration_sec": round(elapsed, 2),
339	        "startup_wait_sec": FIXED_STARTUP_WAIT_SEC,
340	        "probe_mode": "no_spec",
341	        "eval_max_tokens": MAX_TOKENS,
342	        "num_calibration_samples": 1,
343	        "empty_response_debug_records": empty_debug_count,
344	        "total_input_tokens": total_input,
345	        "total_output_tokens": total_output,
346	        "task_breakdown": task_breakdown,
347	        "env": env_info,
348	        "flashinfer_state": final_state,
349	    }
350	
351	    body_lines = [
352	        f"ori_accuracy: {ori_accuracy:.2f}%",
353	        f"overall_accuracy: {summary['overall_accuracy']}%",
354	        f"samples: {len(valid)}",
355	        f"empty_responses: {empty_total}",
356	        f"duration: {elapsed:.0f}s",
357	        f"startup_wait_sec: {FIXED_STARTUP_WAIT_SEC}",
358	        "probe_mode: no_spec",
359	        f"eval_max_tokens: {MAX_TOKENS}",
360	        "num_calibration_samples: 1",
361	        f"empty_response_debug_records: {empty_debug_count}",
362	        f"output_tokens: {total_output}",
363	        "",
364	        "=== Per-task ===",
365	    ]
366	    for task, info in sorted(task_breakdown.items()):
367	        acc = info["correct"] / info["total"] * 100 if info["total"] else 0
368	        body_lines.append(f"  {task:6s}: {info['correct']:.1f}/{info['total']} = {acc:.1f}%  (empty={info['empty']})")
369	
370	    failed = [r for r in valid if r["score"] == 0]
371	    body_lines.append(f"\n=== Failed samples ({len(failed)}) ===")
372	    for r in failed:
373	        body_lines.append(
374	            f"  idx={r['index']} rid={r['rid']} task={r['task']} empty={r['empty']} finish={r['finish_reason']} usage={r['usage']} extracted={r.get('extracted')} pred_len={len(r['prediction'])}"
375	        )
376	
377	    if empty_debug_text:
378	        body_lines.append("\n=== Empty Response Debug ===")
379	        body_lines.extend(empty_debug_text.splitlines())
380	
381	    body_lines.append("\n=== FlashInfer state ===")
382	    body_lines.append(format_state(final_state))
383	    body_lines.append("=== SERVER LOG (first 200 lines) ===")
384	    body_lines.append(read_server_log(script_dir, 200))
385	    body = "\n".join(body_lines)
386	
387	    predictions_json = "\n".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) for r in valid)
388	    predictions_gz = gzip.compress(predictions_json.encode("utf-8"))
389	    summary_json = json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode("utf-8")
390	    server_log_bytes = read_server_log(script_dir).encode("utf-8", errors="replace")
391	    final_state_json = json.dumps(final_state, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2).encode("utf-8")
392	    bundle_buffer = io.BytesIO()
393	    with zipfile.ZipFile(bundle_buffer, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
394	        zf.writestr("predictions.jsonl.gz", predictions_gz)
395	        zf.writestr("summary.json", summary_json)
396	        zf.writestr("server_log.txt", server_log_bytes)
397	        zf.writestr("quant_log.txt", quant_log.encode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
398	        zf.writestr("flashinfer_state_post_eval.json", final_state_json)
399	        if empty_debug_text:
400	            zf.writestr("empty_response_debug.jsonl", empty_debug_text.encode("utf-8"))
401	
402	    print(
403	        f"[probe] predictions: {len(predictions_json)} bytes -> {len(predictions_gz)} bytes gzip, bundle={bundle_buffer.tell()} bytes",
404	        flush=True,
405	    )
406	
407	    send_email(
408	        subject=f"[SOAR probe] 3/3 eval done — acc={ori_accuracy:.2f}% empty={empty_total}",
409	        body=body,
410	        attachments={
411	            "probe_eval_artifacts.zip": bundle_buffer.getvalue(),
412	        },
413	    )
414	
415	    print(f"\n[probe] DONE. ori_accuracy={ori_accuracy:.2f}% empty={empty_total}")
416	    print(body)
417	
418	
419	if __name__ == "__main__":
420	    main()
421
```
